0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e2025/08/30\u003c/code\u003e: 🔥 We open-source the training code of \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/OpenGVLab/InternVL/tree/main/internvl_chat_gpt_oss\"\u003eInternVL3_5-GPT-OSS-20B-A4B\u003c/a\u003e and CascadeRL, which consists of a \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/OpenGVLab/InternVL/blob/main/internvl_chat_gpt_oss/shell/internvl3_5_gpt_oss/internvl3_5_gpt_oss_20b_stage3_mpo.sh\"\u003eoffline RL stage\u003c/a\u003e and a \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/Weiyun1025/verl-internvl\"\u003eonline RL stage\u003c/a\u003e. The training data for these two stages (\u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/datasets/OpenGVLab/MMPR-v1.2\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eMMPR-v1.2\u003c/a\u003e and \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/datasets/OpenGVLab/MMPR-Tiny\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eMMPR-Tiny\u003c/a\u003e) are also open-sourced.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e2025/08/26\u003c/code\u003e: 🚀 We introduce \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/papers/2508.18265\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eInternVL3.5\u003c/a\u003e,  a new family of open-source multimodal models that significantly advances versatility, reasoning capability, and inference efficiency along the InternVL series. Our largest model, i.e., \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/OpenGVLab/InternVL3_5-241B-A28B\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eInternVL3.5-241B-A28B\u003c/a\u003e, attains state-of-the-art results among open-source MLLMs across general multimodal, reasoning, text, and agentic tasks. We also provide a 20B-A4B version (i.e., \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/OpenGVLab/InternVL3_5-GPT-OSS-20B-A4B-Preview\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eInternVL3_5-GPT-OSS-20B-A4B\u003c/a\u003e), which is built up on GPT-OSS-20B-A4B. Notably, we provide two model formats: \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/OpenGVLab/InternVL3_5-GPT-OSS-20B-A4B-Preview#github-format\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003ethe GitHub format\u003c/a\u003e, consistent with prior releases, and \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/OpenGVLab/InternVL3_5-GPT-OSS-20B-A4B-Preview#huggingface-format\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003ethe HF format\u003c/a\u003e, aligned with the official \u003ccode\u003etransformers\u003c/code\u003e standard.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e2025/04/17\u003c/code\u003e: We open-source the \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/OpenGVLab/InternVL/tree/main/internvl_chat/tools/reasoning_data_pipeline\"\u003edata construction pipeline\u003c/a\u003e and \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/OpenGVLab/InternVL/tree/main/internvl_chat/shell/internvl3.0/mpo\"\u003etraining scripts\u003c/a\u003e of \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/papers/2411.10442\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eMPO\u003c/a\u003e and \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/papers/2503.10291\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eVisualPRM\u003c/a\u003e. Additionally, the data construction scripts for \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/OpenGVLab/InternVL/tree/main/internvl_chat/shell/internvl3.0/mpo_data_construction\"\u003eMPO\u003c/a\u003e and \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/OpenGVLab/InternVL/tree/main/internvl_chat/shell/internvl3.0/visualprm_data_construction\"\u003eVisualPRM\u003c/a\u003e are also released for reference.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e2025/04/11\u003c/code\u003e: We introduce \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/collections/OpenGVLab/internvl3-67f7f690be79c2fe9d74fe9d\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eInternVL3\u003c/a\u003e, an advanced multimodal large language model (MLLM) series that demonstrates superior overall performance. InternVL3-78B achieves SoTA performance in both \u003ca href=\"https://rank.opencompass.org.cn/leaderboard-multimodal/?m=REALTIME\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eperception\u003c/a\u003e and \u003ca href=\"https://rank.opencompass.org.cn/leaderboard-multimodal-reasoning/?m=REALTIME\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003ereasoning performance\u003c/a\u003e among open-source MLLMs. The key designs of InternVL3-78B include \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/papers/2412.09616\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eVariable Visual Position Encoding\u003c/a\u003e, \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/papers/2504.10479\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eNative Multimodal Pre-Training\u003c/a\u003e, \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/papers/2411.10442\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eMixed Preference Optimization\u003c/a\u003e, and \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/papers/2503.10291\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eMultimodal Test-Time Scaling\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e2025/03/13\u003c/code\u003e: We introduce \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/OpenGVLab/VisualPRM-8B\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eVisualPRM\u003c/a\u003e, an advanced multimodal Process Reward Model (PRM) with 8B parameters, which improves the overall reasoning performance of InternVL2.5-8B and InternVL2.5-78B by 8.4 and 5.9 points, respectively. The training data for this model, termed \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/datasets/OpenGVLab/VisualPRM400K\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eVisualPRM400K\u003c/a\u003e, is also open-sourced. Please refer to our \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/papers/2503.10291\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003epaper\u003c/a\u003e and \u003ca href=\"https://internvl.github.io/blog/2025-03-13-VisualPRM/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eproject page\u003c/a\u003e for more details.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e2024/12/20\u003c/code\u003e: We release the \u003ca href=\"https://internvl.github.io/blog/2024-12-20-InternVL-2.5-MPO/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eInternVL2.5-MPO\u003c/a\u003e, which is finetuned with \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/papers/2411.10442\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eMixed Preference Optimization\u003c/a\u003e on \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/datasets/OpenGVLab/MMPR-v1.1\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eMMPR-v1.1\u003c/a\u003e. \u003cstrong\u003eThe resulting models outperform their counterparts without MPO by an average of 2 points across all model scales on the OpenCompass leaderboard.\u003c/strong\u003e These models are available at \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/collections/OpenGVLab/internvl25-mpo-6753fed98cd828219b12f849\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eHF link\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e2024/12/17\u003c/code\u003e: \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/PaddlePaddle/PaddleMIX/tree/develop/paddlemix/examples/internvl2\"\u003eInternVL2/2.5\u003c/a\u003e is supported in \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/PaddlePaddle/PaddleMIX\"\u003ePaddleMIX\u003c/a\u003e by Paddle Team.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e2024/12/05\u003c/code\u003e: We release the \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/collections/OpenGVLab/internvl-25-673e1019b66e2218f68d7c1c\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eInternVL2.5\u003c/a\u003e, an advanced multimodal large language model (MLLM) series with parameter coverage ranging from 1B to 78B. \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/OpenGVLab/InternVL2_5-78B\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eInternVL2_5-78B\u003c/a\u003e is the first open-source MLLMs to achieve over \u003cstrong\u003e70%\u003c/strong\u003e on the \u003cstrong\u003eMMMU benchmark\u003c/strong\u003e, matching the performance of leading closed-source commercial models like GPT-4o. These models are available at \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/collections/OpenGVLab/internvl-25-673e1019b66e2218f68d7c1c\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eHF link\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e2024/11/14\u003c/code\u003e: We introduce \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/datasets/OpenGVLab/MMPR\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eMMPR\u003c/a\u003e, a high-quality, large-scale multimodal reasoning preference dataset, and \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/OpenGVLab/InternVL/tree/main/internvl_chat/shell/internvl2.0_mpo\"\u003eMPO\u003c/a\u003e, an effective preference optimization algorithm. The resulting model, \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/OpenGVLab/InternVL2-8B-MPO\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eInternVL2-8B-MPO\u003c/a\u003e, achieves an accuracy of 67.0 on MathVista. Please refer to our \u003ca href=\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.10442\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003epaper\u003c/a\u003e, \u003ca href=\"https://internvl.github.io/blog/2024-11-14-InternVL-2.0-MPO/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eproject page\u003c/a\u003e and \u003ca href=\"https://internvl.readthedocs.io/en/latest/internvl2.0/preference_optimization.html\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003edocument\u003c/a\u003e for more details.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003eMore News\u003c/summary\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e2024/10/21\u003c/code\u003e: We release the Mini-InternVL series. These models achieve impressive performance with minimal size: the 4B model achieves 90% of the performance with just 5% of the model size. For more details, please check our \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/OpenGVLab/InternVL/tree/main/internvl_chat/shell/mini_internvl\"\u003eproject page\u003c/a\u003e and \u003ca href=\"https://internvl.readthedocs.io/en/latest/internvl2.0/domain_adaptation.html\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003edocument\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e2024/08/01\u003c/code\u003e: The \u003ca href=\"https://chartmimic.github.io/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eChartmimic\u003c/a\u003e team evaluated the InternVL2 series models on their benchmark. The InternVL2-26B and 76B models achieved the top two performances among open-source models, with the InternVL2 76B model surpassing GeminiProVision and exhibiting comparable results to Claude-3-opus.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e2024/08/01\u003c/code\u003e: InternVL2-Pro achieved the SOTA performance among open-source models on the \u003ca href=\"https://charxiv.github.io/#leaderboard\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eCharXiv\u003c/a\u003e dataset, surpassing many closed-source models such as GPT-4V, Gemini 1.5 Flash, and Claude 3 Sonnet.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e2024/07/24\u003c/code\u003e: The \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/JUNJIE99/MLVU\"\u003eMLVU\u003c/a\u003e team evaluated InternVL-1.5 on their benchmark. The average performance on the multiple-choice task was 50.4%, while the performance on the generative tasks was 4.02. The performance on the multiple-choice task ranked #1 among all open-source MLLMs.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e2024/07/04\u003c/code\u003e: We release the \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/collections/OpenGVLab/internvl-20-667d3961ab5eb12c7ed1463e\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eInternVL2 series\u003c/a\u003e. InternVL2-Pro achieved a 62.0% accuracy on the MMMU benchmark, matching the performance of leading closed-source commercial models like GPT-4o.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e2024/07/18\u003c/code\u003e: InternVL2-40B achieved SOTA performance among open-source models on the \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/BradyFU/Video-MME\"\u003eVideo-MME\u003c/a\u003e dataset, scoring 61.2 when inputting 16 frames and 64.4 when inputting 32 frames. It significantly outperforms other open-source models and is the closest open-source model to GPT-4o mini.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e2024/07/18\u003c/code\u003e: InternVL2-Pro achieved the SOTA performance on the \u003ca href=\"https://rrc.cvc.uab.es/?ch=17\u0026amp;com=evaluation\u0026amp;task=1\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eDocVQA\u003c/a\u003e and \u003ca href=\"https://rrc.cvc.uab.es/?ch=17\u0026amp;com=evaluation\u0026amp;task=3\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eInfoVQA\u003c/a\u003e benchmarks.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e2024/06/19\u003c/code\u003e: We propose Needle In A Multimodal Haystack (\u003ca href=\"https://github.com/OpenGVLab/MM-NIAH\"\u003eMM-NIAH\u003c/a\u003e), the first benchmark designed to systematically evaluate the capability of existing MLLMs to comprehend long multimodal documents.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e2024/05/30\u003c/code\u003e: We release \u003ca href=\"https://sharegpt4o.github.io/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eShareGPT-4o\u003c/a\u003e, a large-scale dataset that we plan to open-source with 200K images, 10K videos, and 10K audios with detailed descriptions.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e2024/05/28\u003c/code\u003e: Thanks to the \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/InternLM/lmdeploy\"\u003elmdeploy\u003c/a\u003e team for providing AWQ quantization support. The 4-bit model is available at \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/OpenGVLab/InternVL-Chat-V1-5-AWQ\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eOpenGVLab/InternVL-Chat-V1-5-AWQ\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e2024/05/13\u003c/code\u003e: InternVL 1.0 can now be used as the \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/OpenGVLab/InternVL-14B-224px\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003etext encoder\u003c/a\u003e for diffusion models to support multilingual generation natively in over 110 languages worldwide. See \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/mulanai/MuLan\"\u003eMuLan\u003c/a\u003e for more details.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e2024/04/18\u003c/code\u003e: InternVL-Chat-V1-5 has been released at \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/OpenGVLab/InternVL-Chat-V1-5\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eHF link\u003c/a\u003e, approaching the performance of GPT-4V and Gemini Pro on various benchmarks like MMMU, DocVQA, ChartQA, MathVista, etc.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e2024/02/27\u003c/code\u003e: InternVL is accepted by CVPR 2024 (Oral)! 🎉\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e2024/02/21\u003c/code\u003e: \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/OpenGVLab/InternVL-Chat-V1-2-Plus\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eInternVL-Chat-V1-2-Plus\u003c/a\u003e achieved SOTA performance on MathVista (59.9), MMBench (83.8), and MMVP (58.7). See our \u003ca href=\"https://internvl.github.io/blog/2024-02-21-InternVL-1.2/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eblog\u003c/a\u003e for more details.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e2024/02/12\u003c/code\u003e: InternVL-Chat-V1-2 has been released. It achieves 51.6 on MMMU val and 82.3 on MMBench test. For more details, please refer to our \u003ca href=\"https://internvl.github.io/blog/2024-02-21-InternVL-1.2/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eblog\u003c/a\u003e and \u003ca href=\"/OpenGVLab/InternVL/blob/main/internvl_chat#prepare-training-datasets\"\u003eSFT data\u003c/a\u003e. The model is now available on \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/OpenGVLab/InternVL-Chat-V1-2\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eHuggingFace\u003c/a\u003e, and both training / evaluation data and scripts are open-sourced.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e2024/01/24\u003c/code\u003e: InternVL-Chat-V1-1 is released, it supports Chinese and has stronger OCR capability, see \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/OpenGVLab/InternVL-Chat-V1-1\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003ehere\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e2024/01/16\u003c/code\u003e: We release our \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/OpenGVLab/InternVL-MMDetSeg\"\u003ecustomized mmcv/mmsegmentation/mmdetection code\u003c/a\u003e, integrated with DeepSpeed, which can be used for training large-scale detection and segmentation models.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"import torch\nfrom PIL import Image\nfrom transformers import AutoModel, CLIPImageProcessor\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer\n\n\nmodel = AutoModel.from_pretrained(\n    'OpenGVLab/InternVL-14B-224px',\n    torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\n    low_cpu_mem_usage=True,\n    trust_remote_code=True).cuda().eval()\n\nimage_processor = CLIPImageProcessor.from_pretrained('OpenGVLab/InternVL-14B-224px')\n\ntokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(\n    'OpenGVLab/InternVL-14B-224px', use_fast=False, add_eos_token=True)\ntokenizer.pad_token_id = 0  # set pad_token_id to 0\n\nimages = [\n    Image.open('./examples/image1.jpg').convert('RGB'),\n    Image.open('./examples/image2.jpg').convert('RGB'),\n    Image.open('./examples/image3.jpg').convert('RGB')\n]\nprefix = 'summarize:'\ntexts = [\n    prefix + 'a photo of a red panda',  # English\n    prefix + '一张熊猫的照片',  # Chinese\n    prefix + '二匹の猫の写真'  # Japanese\n]\n\npixel_values = image_processor(images=images, return_tensors='pt').pixel_values\npixel_values = pixel_values.to(torch.bfloat16).cuda()\ninput_ids = tokenizer(texts, return_tensors='pt', max_length=80,\n                      truncation=True, padding='max_length').input_ids.cuda()\n\n# InternVL-C\nlogits_per_image, logits_per_text = model(\n    image=pixel_values, text=input_ids, mode='InternVL-C')\nprobs = logits_per_image.softmax(dim=-1)\n# tensor([[9.9609e-01, 5.2185e-03, 6.0070e-08],\n#         [2.2949e-02, 9.7656e-01, 5.9903e-06],\n#         [3.2932e-06, 7.4863e-05, 1.0000e+00]], device='cuda:0',\n#        dtype=torch.bfloat16, grad_fn=\u0026lt;SoftmaxBackward0\u0026gt;)\n\n# InternVL-G\nlogits_per_image, logits_per_text = model(\n    image=pixel_values, text=input_ids, mode='InternVL-G')\nprobs = logits_per_image.softmax(dim=-1)\n# tensor([[9.9609e-01, 3.1738e-03, 3.6322e-08],\n#         [8.6060e-03, 9.9219e-01, 2.8759e-06],\n#         [1.7583e-06, 3.1233e-05, 1.0000e+00]], device='cuda:0',\n#        dtype=torch.bfloat16, grad_fn=\u0026lt;SoftmaxBackward0\u0026gt;)\n\n# please set add_eos_token to False for generation\ntokenizer.add_eos_token = False\nimage = Image.open('./examples/image1.jpg').convert('RGB')\npixel_values = image_processor(images=image, return_tensors='pt').pixel_values\npixel_values = pixel_values.to(torch.bfloat16).cuda()\n\ntokenized = tokenizer(\u0026quot;English caption:\u0026quot;, return_tensors='pt')\npred = model.generate(\n    pixel_values=pixel_values,\n    input_ids=tokenized.input_ids.cuda(),\n    attention_mask=tokenized.attention_mask.cuda(),\n    num_beams=5,\n    min_new_tokens=8,\n)\ncaption = tokenizer.decode(pred[0].cpu(), skip_special_tokens=True).strip()\n# English caption: a red panda class=\"pl-s1\"\u003ecaption\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003etokenizer\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eTrue\u003c/span\u003e).\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003estrip\u003c/span\u003e()\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e# English caption: a red panda sitting on top of a wooden platform\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n  \u003csummary\u003eusing InternVL 2.5 for multimodal chat (click to expand)\u003c/summary\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eHere, we take the smaller \u003ccode\u003eOpenGVLab/InternVL2_5-8B\u003c/code\u003e as an example:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"import numpy as np\nimport torch\nimport torchvision.transforms as T\nfrom decord import VideoReader, cpu\nfrom PIL import Image\nfrom torchvision.transforms.functional import InterpolationMode\nfrom transformers import AutoModel, AutoTokenizer\n\nIMAGENET_MEAN = (0.485, 0.456, 0.406)\nIMAGENET_STD = (0.229, 0.224, 0.225)\n\ndef build_transform(input_size):\n    MEAN, STD = IMAGENET_MEAN, IMAGENET_STD\n    transform = T.Compose([\n        T.Lambda(lambda img: img.convert('RGB') if img.mode != 'RGB' else img),\n        T.Resize((input_size, input_size), interpolation=InterpolationMode.BICUBIC),\n        T.ToTensor(),\n        T.Normalize(mean=MEAN, std=STD)\n    ])\n    return transform\n\ndef find_closest_aspect_ratio(aspect_ratio, target_ratios, width, height, image_size):\n    best_ratio_diff = float('inf')\n    best_ratio = (1, 1)\n    area = width * height\n    for ratio in target_ratios:\n        target_aspect_ratio = ratio[0] / ratio[1]\n        ratio_diff = abs(aspect_ratio - target_aspect_ratio)\n        if ratio_diff \u0026lt; best_ratio_diff:\n            best_ratio_diff = ratio_diff\n            best_ratio = ratio\n        elif ratio_diff == best_ratio_diff:\n            if area \u0026gt; 0.5 * image_size * image_size * ratio[0] * ratio[1]:\n                best_ratio = ratio\n    return best_ratio\n\ndef dynamic_preprocess(image, min_num=1, max_num=12, image_size=448, use_thumbnail=False):\n    orig_width, orig_height = image.size\n    aspect_ratio = orig_width / orig_height\n\n    # calculate the existing image aspect ratio\n    target_ratios = set(\n        (i, j) for n in range(min_num, max_num + 1) for i in range(1, n + 1) for j in range(1, n + 1) if\n        i * j \u0026lt;= max_num and i * j \u0026gt;= min_num)\n    target_ratios = sorted(target_ratios, key=lambda x: x[0] * x[1])\n\n    # find the closest aspect ratio to the target\n    target_aspect_ratio = find_closest_aspect_ratio(\n        aspect_ratio, target_ratios, orig_width, orig_height, image_size)\n\n    # calculate the target width and height\n    target_width = image_size * target_aspect_ratio[0]\n    target_height = image_size * target_aspect_ratio[1]\n    blocks = target_aspect_ratio[0] * target_aspect_ratio[1]\n\n    # resize the image\n    resized_img = image.resize((target_width, target_height))\n    processed_images = []\n    for i in range(blocks):\n        box = (\n            (i % (target_width // image_size)) * image_size,\n            (i // (target_width // image_size)) * image_size,\n            ((i % (target_width // image_size)) + 1) * image_size,\n            ((i // (target_width // image_size)) + 1) * image_size\n        )\n        # split the image\n        split_img = resized_img.crop(box)\n        processed_images.append(split_img)\n    assert len(processed_images) == blocks\n    if use_thumbnail and len(processed_images) != 1:\n        thumbnail_img = image.resize((image_size, image_size))\n        processed_images.append(thumbnail_img)\n    return processed_images\n\ndef load_image(image_file, input_size=448, max_num=12):\n    image = Image.open(image_file).convert('RGB')\n    transform = build_transform(input_size=input_size)\n    images = dynamic_preprocess(image, image_size=input_size, use_thumbnail=True, max_num=max_num)\n    pixel_values = [transform(image) for image in images]\n    pixel_values = torch.stack(pixel_values)\n    return pixel_values\n\n# If you have an 80G A100 GPU, you can put the entire model on a single GPU.\n# Otherwise, you need to load a model using multiple GPUs, please refer to the `Multiple GPUs` section.\npath = 'OpenGVLab/InternVL2_5-8B'\nmodel = AutoModel.from_pretrained(\n    path,\n    torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\n    low_cpu_mem_usage=True,\n    trust_remote_code=True).eval().cuda()\ntokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(path, trust_remote_code=True, use_fast=False)\n\n# set the max number of tiles in `max_num`\npixel_values = load_image('./examples/image1.jpg', max_num=12).to(torch.bfloat16).cuda()\ngeneration_config = dict(max_new_tokens=1024, do_sample=False)\n\n# pure-text conversation (纯文本对话)\nquestion = 'Hello, who are you?'\nresponse, history = model.chat(tokenizer, None, question, generation_config, history=None, return_history=True)\nprint(f'User: {question}\\nAssistant: {response}')\n\nquestion = 'Can you tell me a story?'\nresponse, history = model.chat(tokenizer, None, question, generation_config, history=history, return_history=True)\nprint(f'User: {question}\\nAssistant: {response}')\n\n# single-image single-round conversation (单图单轮对话)\nquestion = '\u0026lt;image\u0026gt;\\nPlease describe the image shortly.'\nresponse = model.chat(tokenizer, pixel_values, question, generation_config)\nprint(f'User: {question}\\nAssistant: {response}')\n\n# single-image multi-round conversation (单图多轮对话)\nquestion = '\u0026lt;image\u0026gt;\\nPlease describe the image in detail.'\nresponse, history = model.chat(tokenizer, pixel_values, question, generation_config, history=None, return_history=True)\nprint(f'User: {question}\\nAssistant: {response}')\n\nquestion = 'Please write a poem according to the image.'\nresponse, history = model.chat(tokenizer, pixel_values, question, generation_config, history=history, return_history=True)\nprint(f'User: {question}\\nAssistant: {response}')\n\n# multi-image multi-round conversation, combined images (多图多轮对话，拼接图像)\npixel_values1 = load_image('./examples/image1.jpg', max_num=12).to(torch.bfloat16).cuda()\npixel_values2 = load_image('./examples/image2.jpg', max_num=12).to(torch.bfloat16).cuda()\npixel_values = torch.cat((pixel_values1, pixel_values2), dim=0)\n\nquestion = '\u0026lt;image\u0026gt;\\nDescribe the two images in detail.'\nresponse, history = model.chat(tokenizer, pixel_values, question, generation_config,\n                               history=None, return_history=True)\nprint(f'User: {question}\\nAssistant: {response}')\n\nquestion = 'What are the similarities and differences between these two images.'\nresponse, history = model.chat(tokenizer, pixel_values, question, generation_config,\n                               history=history, return_history=True)\nprint(f'User: {question}\\nAssistant: {response}')\n\n# multi-image multi-round conversation, separate images (多图多轮对话，独立图像)\npixel_values1 = load_image('./examples/image1.jpg', max_num=12).to(torch.bfloat16).cuda()\npixel_values2 = load_image('./examples/image2.jpg', max_num=12).to(torch.bfloat16).cuda()\npixel_values = torch.cat((pixel_values1, pixel_values2), dim=0)\nnum_patches_list = [pixel_values1.size(0), pixel_values2.size(0)]\n\nquestion = 'Image-1: \u0026lt;image\u0026gt;\\nImage-2: \u0026lt;image\u0026gt;\\nDescribe the two images in detail.'\nresponse, history = model.chat(tokenizer, pixel_values, question, generation_config,\n                               num_patches_list=num_patches_list,\n                               history=None, return_history=True)\nprint(f'User: {question}\\nAssistant: {response}')\n\nquestion = 'What are the similarities and differences between these two images.'\nresponse, history = model.chat(tokenizer, pixel_values, question, generation_config,\n                               num_patches_list=num_patches_list,\n                               history=history, return_history=True)\nprint(f'User: {question}\\nAssistant: {response}')\n\n# batch inference, single image per sample (单图批处理)\npixel_values1 = load_image('./examples/image1.jpg', max_num=12).to(torch.bfloat16).cuda()\npixel_values2 = load_image('./examples/image2.jpg', max_num=12).to(torch.bfloat16).cuda()\nnum_patches_list = [pixel_values1.size(0), pixel_values2.size(0)]\npixel_values = torch.cat((pixel_values1, pixel_values2), dim=0)\n\nquestions = ['\u0026lt;image\u0026gt;\\nDescribe the image in detail.'] * len(num_patches_list)\nresponses = model.batch_chat(tokenizer, pixel_values,\n                             num_patches_list=num_patches_list,\n                             questions=questions,\n                             generation_config=generation_config)\nfor question, response in zip(questions, responses):\n    print(f'User: {question}\\nAssistant: {response}')\n\n# video multi-round conversation (视频多轮对话)\ndef get_index(bound, fps, max_frame, first_idx=0, num_segments=32):\n    if bound:\n        start, end = bound[0], bound[1]\n    else:\n        start, end = -100000, 100000\n    start_idx = max(first_idx, round(start * fps))\n    end_idx = min(round(end * fps), max_frame)\n    seg_size = float(end_idx - start_idx) / num_segments\n    frame_indices = np.array([\n        int(start_idx + (seg_size / 2) + np.round(seg_size * idx))\n        for idx in range(num_segments)\n    ])\n    return frame_indices\n\ndef load_video(video_path, bound=None, input_size=448, max_num=1, num_segments=32):\n    vr = VideoReader(video_path, ctx=cpu(0), num_threads=1)\n    max_frame = len(vr) - 1\n    fps = float(vr.get_avg_fps())\n\n    pixel_values_list, num_patches_list = [], []\n    transform = build_transform(input_size=input_size)\n    frame_indices = get_index(bound, fps, max_frame, first_idx=0, num_segments=num_segments)\n    for frame_index in frame_indices:\n        img = Image.fromarray(vr[frame_index].asnumpy()).convert('RGB')\n        img = dynamic_preprocess(img, image_size=input_size, use_thumbnail=True, max_num=max_num)\n        pixel_values = [transform(tile) for tile in img]\n        pixel_values = torch.stack(pixel_values)\n        num_patches_list.append(pixel_values.shape[0])\n        pixel_values_list.append(pixel_values)\n    pixel_values = torch.cat(pixel_values_list)\n    return pixel_values, num_patches_list\n\nvideo_path = './examples/red-panda.mp4'\npixel_values, num_patches_list = load_video(video_path, num_segments=8, max_num=1)\npixel_values = pixel_values.to(torch.bfloat16).cuda()\nvideo_prefix = ''.join([f'Frame-{i+1}: \u0026lt;image\u0026gt;\\n' for i in range(len(num_patches_list))])\nquestion = video_prefix + 'What is the red panda doing?'\n# Frame1: \u0026lt;image\u0026gt;\\nFrame2: \u0026lt;image\u0026gt;\\n...\\nFrame8: \u0026lt;image\u0026gt;\\n{question}\nresponse, history = model.chat(tokenizer, pixel_values, question, generation_config,\n                               num_patches_list=num_patches_list, history=None, return_history=True)\nprint(f'User: {question}\\nAssistant: {response}')\n\nquestion = 'Describe this video in detail.'\nresponse, history = model.chat(tokenizer, pixel_values, question, generation_config,\n                               num_patches_list=num_patches_list, history=history, return_history=True)\nprint(f'User: {question}\\nAssistant: {response}')\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan
